In this small cohort setting, trimodal early fusion achieves an exploratory Composite Score (CS = 0.854), with a controlled $Δ$CS of +0.011 over the bimodal baseline on identical patients, though this difference is not statistically significant (p = 0.250, permutation test). MRI achieves reasonable unimodal discrimination (CS = 0.755) but does not substantially improve bimodal pairs, while providing measurable uplift in the three-way combination. All MRI containing experiments are constrained to 19 test patients, yielding wide bootstrap confidence intervals (e.g. [0.400,1.000]) that preclude definitive conclusions. These findings provide preliminary evidence that a third imaging modality may add prognostic value even with limited sample sizes, and that additional modalities require sufficient multimodal context to contribute effectively.

</details>


### [115] [SurgNavAR: An Augmented Reality Surgical Navigation Framework for Optical See-Through Head Mounted Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29990)
*Abdullah Thabit,Mohamed Benmahdjoub,Rafiuddin Jinabade,Hizirwan S. Salim,Marie-Lise C. van Veelen,Mark G. van Vledder,Eppo B. Wolvius,Theo van Walsum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个集成化的头戴式AR手术导航框架，支持患者和手术器械追踪、工具校准、图像配准等功能，在HoloLens 2和Magic Leap 2上验证了针插入和肋骨骨折定位的导航精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有头戴式AR设备作为独立手术导航系统使用时，需要集成患者定位、器械追踪、术前影像配准等多种技术，但集成过程复杂且需要专业知识，阻碍了该领域的科研进展

Method: 开发了一个可配置的AR手术导航框架，使用2D图案作为参考标记追踪患者和手术器械，支持枢轴和参考校准技术进行工具校准，采用点匹配和手动定位实现图像到患者的配准

Result: 在HoloLens 2和Magic Leap 2设备上评估，实现了平均1mm的工具尖端校准精度、3mm的配准精度，在两个手术用例中靶向精度低于5mm

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个易于使用、可配置的头戴式AR手术导航工具，可扩展和适应多种手术应用，代码已公开在GitHub上

Abstract: Augmented reality (AR) devices with head mounted displays (HMDs) facilitate the direct superimposition of 3D preoperative imaging data onto the patient during surgery. To use an HMD-AR device as a stand-alone surgical navigation system, the device should be able to locate the patient and surgical instruments, align preoperative imaging data with the patient, and visualize navigation data in real time during surgery. Whereas some of the technologies required for this are known, integration in such devices is cumbersome and requires specific knowledge and expertise, hampering scientific progress in this field. This work therefore aims to present and evaluate an integrated HMD-based AR surgical navigation framework that is adaptable to diverse surgical applications. The framework tracks 2D patterns as reference markers attached to the patient and surgical instruments. It allows for the calibration of surgical tools using pivot and reference-based calibration techniques. It enables image-to-patient registration using point-based matching and manual positioning. The integrated functionalities of the framework are evaluated on two HMD devices, the HoloLens 2 and Magic Leap 2, with two surgical use cases being evaluated in a phantom setup: AR-guided needle insertion and rib fracture localization. The framework was able to achieve a mean tooltip calibration accuracy of 1 mm, a registration accuracy of 3 mm, and a targeting accuracy below 5 mm on the two surgical use cases. The framework presents an easy-to-use configurable tool for HMD-based AR surgical navigation, which can be extended and adapted to many surgical applications. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/abdullahthabit/SurgNavAR.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking PhD-Level Coding in 3D Geometric Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30038)
*Wenyi Li,Renkai Luo,Yue Yu,Huan-ang Gao,Mingju Gao,Li Yuan,Chaoyou Fu,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCodeBench是一个评估3D几何视觉编程能力的博士级基准测试，包含从代表性论文中筛选的核心3D几何组件实现任务，最佳模型GPT-5仅达到36.6%通过率，显示当前能力与可靠3D科学编程之间存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助编码模型在处理复杂3D几何视觉任务时仍存在困难，而如果模型能够可靠地编写此类代码，将显著改变研究社区的工作流程。为了衡量向这一目标的进展，需要建立一个专门的评估基准。

Method: 从近期代表性论文的官方仓库中提取候选函数，经过人工筛选选择核心3D几何组件，为每个目标生成多样化的边缘情况单元测试，实现全自动、可复现的评分。将任务组织为两级层次：通用3D能力（几何变换和力学/光学公式）和研究能力（新算法实现和几何逻辑路由）。

Result: 评估了8个代表性的开源和闭源模型，最佳模型GPT-5仅达到36.6%通过率。研究导向任务明显更难，且上下文消融实验显示"更多论文文本"并不总是更好：在方法部分截断的输入在统计上优于完整论文输入。

Conclusion: GeoCodeBench为从通用编码向可信赖的3D几何视觉编码的进展提供了一个严格的测试平台，揭示了当前模型在科学长文本理解和复杂几何编程方面的未解决挑战。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding has rapidly reshaped software practice and research workflows, yet today's models still struggle to produce correct code for complex 3D geometric vision. If models could reliably write such code, the research of our community would change substantially. To measure progress toward that goal, we introduce GeoCodeBench, a PhD-level benchmark that evaluates coding for 3D vision. Each problem is a fill-in-the-function implementation task curated from representative papers at recent venues: we first let a tool propose candidate functions from official repositories, then perform careful human screening to select core 3D geometric components. For every target, we generate diverse, edge-case unit tests, enabling fully automatic, reproducible scoring. We evaluate eight representative open- and closed-source models to reflect the current ecosystem. The best model, GPT-5, attains only 36.6% pass rate, revealing a large gap between current capabilities and dependable 3D scientific coding. GeoCodeBench organizes tasks into a two-level hierarchy: General 3D capability (geometric transformations and mechanics/optics formulation) and Research capability (novel algorithm implementation and geometric logic routing). Scores are positively correlated across these axes, but research-oriented tasks are markedly harder. Context ablations further show that "more paper text" is not always better: cutting off at the Method section statistically outperforms full-paper inputs, highlighting unresolved challenges in long-context scientific comprehension. Together, these findings position GeoCodeBench as a rigorous testbed for advancing from generic coding to trustworthy 3D geometric vision coding.

</details>


### [117] [Video Models Reason Early: Exploiting Plan Commitment for Maze Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30043)
*Kaleb Newman,Tyler Zhu,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在迷宫求解中表现出早期规划承诺特性，路径长度而非障碍密度是迷宫难度的主要预测因子，通过早期规划链式方法可显著提升长序列推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型展现出解决迷宫和谜题等推理能力，但对其在生成过程中的推理机制了解甚少。本文旨在通过2D迷宫求解这一受控测试平台，首次研究视频模型的内部规划动态

Method: 使用2D迷宫求解作为受控测试平台，分析视频扩散模型的内部规划动态。提出Chaining with Early Planning (ChEaP)方法，该方法仅在有前景的早期规划种子上投入计算资源，并通过链式连接处理复杂迷宫

Result: 发现两个关键现象：1) 早期规划承诺：模型在前几个去噪步骤中即确定高层运动规划，后续去噪仅改变视觉细节而不影响基本轨迹；2) 路径长度（而非障碍密度）是迷宫难度的主要预测因子，存在12步的失败阈值。ChEaP方法将长视野迷宫的准确率从7%提升至67%，在Frozen Lake和VR-Bench困难任务上整体提升2.5倍

Conclusion: 当前视频模型具有比先前认识更深的推理能力，通过更好的推理时缩放可以更可靠地激发这些能力。早期规划承诺现象和路径长度作为难度主要预测因子的发现，为理解和改进视频模型的推理能力提供了新视角

Abstract: Video diffusion models exhibit emergent reasoning capabilities like solving mazes and puzzles, yet little is understood about how they reason during generation. We take a first step towards understanding this and study the internal planning dynamics of video models using 2D maze solving as a controlled testbed. Our investigations reveal two findings. Our first finding is early plan commitment: video diffusion models commit to a high-level motion plan within the first few denoising steps, after which further denoising alters visual details but not the underlying trajectory. Our second finding is that path length, not obstacle density, is the dominant predictor of maze difficulty, with a sharp failure threshold at 12 steps. This means video models can only reason over long mazes by chaining together multiple sequential generations. To demonstrate the practical benefits of our findings, we introduce Chaining with Early Planning, or ChEaP, which only spends compute on seeds with promising early plans and chains them together to tackle complex mazes. This improves accuracy from 7% to 67% on long-horizon mazes and by 2.5x overall on hard tasks in Frozen Lake and VR-Bench across Wan2.2-14B and HunyuanVideo-1.5. Our analysis reveals that current video models possess deeper reasoning capabilities than previously recognized, which can be elicited more reliably with better inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [118] [OmniRoam: World Wandering via Long-Horizon Panoramic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30045)
*Yuheng Liu,Xin Lin,Xinke Li,Baihan Yang,Chen Wang,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Hao Tan,Kai Zhang,Xiaohui Xie,Zifan Shi,Yiwei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniRoam是一个可控的全景视频生成框架，通过利用全景表示丰富的每帧场景覆盖和固有的长时空一致性，实现长时场景漫游。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖透视视频模型，只能合成场景的有限观测，导致完整性和全局一致性问题。需要一种能够生成长时、高保真场景漫游视频的方法。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：预览阶段使用轨迹控制视频生成模型从输入图像/视频创建场景概览；精炼阶段将视频时间扩展和空间上采样，生成长范围高分辨率视频。使用合成和真实世界捕获的全景视频数据集进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在视觉质量、可控性和长时场景一致性方面均优于现有方法，支持实时视频生成和3D重建等扩展应用。

Conclusion: OmniRoam通过全景表示解决了传统透视视频模型的局限性，实现了高质量、可控的长时场景漫游生成，为视频生成领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Modeling scenes using video generation models has garnered growing research interest in recent years. However, most existing approaches rely on perspective video models that synthesize only limited observations of a scene, leading to issues of completeness and global consistency. We propose OmniRoam, a controllable panoramic video generation framework that exploits the rich per-frame scene coverage and inherent long-term spatial and temporal consistency of panoramic representation, enabling long-horizon scene wandering. Our framework begins with a preview stage, where a trajectory-controlled video generation model creates a quick overview of the scene from a given input image or video. Then, in the refine stage, this video is temporally extended and spatially upsampled to produce long-range, high-resolution videos, thus enabling high-fidelity world wandering. To train our model, we introduce two panoramic video datasets that incorporate both synthetic and real-world captured videos. Experiments show that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of visual quality, controllability, and long-term scene consistency, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We further showcase several extensions of this framework, including real-time video generation and 3D reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam.

</details>


### [119] [Few-shot Writer Adaptation via Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29450)
*Tom Simon,Stephane Nicolas,Pierrick Tranouez,Clement Chatelain,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态上下文学习的上下文驱动HTR框架，实现无需参数更新的推理时书写者自适应，仅需少量目标书写者示例即可适应新书写风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTR模型在标准基准上表现良好，但对训练数据中代表性不足的特定书写风格适应能力差。传统书写者自适应方法需要离线微调或推理时参数更新，涉及梯度计算和反向传播，计算成本高且需要仔细的超参数调优。

Method: 提出受多模态上下文学习启发的上下文驱动HTR框架，使用紧凑的8M参数CNN-Transformer架构，支持少样本上下文自适应。结合上下文驱动和标准OCR训练策略实现互补改进。

Result: 在IAM和RIMES数据集上分别达到3.92%和2.34%的字符错误率，超越了所有无需推理时参数更新的书写者无关HTR模型。

Conclusion: 上下文驱动HTR框架通过少量示例实现推理时书写者自适应，无需参数更新，为处理未见和异常书写风格提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: While state-of-the-art Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) models perform well on standard benchmarks, they frequently struggle with writers exhibiting highly specific styles that are underrepresented in the training data. To handle unseen and atypical writers, writer adaptation techniques personalize HTR models to individual handwriting styles. Leading writer adaptation methods require either offline fine-tuning or parameter updates at inference time, both involving gradient computation and backpropagation, which increase computational costs and demand careful hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we propose a novel context-driven HTR framework3 inspired by multimodal in-context learning, enabling inference-time writer adaptation using only a few examples from the target writer without any parameter updates. We further demonstrate the impact of context length, design a compact 8M-parameter CNN-Transformer that enables few-shot in-context adaptation, and show that combining context-driven and standard OCR training strategies leads to complementary improvements. Experiments on IAM and RIMES validate our approach with Character Error Rates of 3.92% and 2.34%, respectively, surpassing all writer-independent HTR models without requiring any parameter updates at inference time.

</details>


### [120] [Training deep learning based dynamic MR image reconstruction using synthetic fractals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29922)
*Anirudh Raman,Olivier Jaubert,Mark Wrobel,Tina Yao,Ruaraidh Campbell,Rebecca Baker,Ruta Virsinskaite,Daniel Knight,Michael Quail,Jennifer Steeden,Vivek Muthurangu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用合成分形数据训练深度学习模型进行动态MRI重建，避免真实心脏MRI数据的隐私、许可和可用性限制，性能与使用真实心脏数据训练的模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决心脏MRI训练数据集面临的隐私、许可和可用性限制问题，为深度学习重建模型提供开放、可扩展的替代训练数据来源。

Method: 使用四元数Julia分形生成2D+时间图像训练数据集，模拟多线圈MRI采集生成配对的全采样和径向欠采样k空间数据，训练3D UNet深度学习伪影抑制模型，并与使用真实心脏MRI数据训练的相同模型进行比较。

Result: 分形数据训练的模型(F-DL)与心脏MRI数据训练的模型(CMR-DL)在定性排名上无显著差异(p=0.9)，两者均优于压缩感知(CS)和低秩深度图像先验(LR-DIP)(p<0.001)。心室容积和功能测量与参考电影MRI相比无显著偏差，具有可接受的界限一致性。

Conclusion: 使用合成分形数据训练的深度学习模型能够重建实时心脏MRI，其图像质量和临床测量结果与使用真实心脏MRI数据训练的模型相当。分形训练数据为临床数据集提供了开放、可扩展的替代方案，可能促进更通用的动态MRI深度学习重建模型的发展。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether synthetically generated fractal data can be used to train deep learning (DL) models for dynamic MRI reconstruction, thereby avoiding the privacy, licensing, and availability limitations associated with cardiac MR training datasets. Methods: A training dataset was generated using quaternion Julia fractals to produce 2D+time images. Multi-coil MRI acquisition was simulated to generate paired fully sampled and radially undersampled k-space data. A 3D UNet deep artefact suppression model was trained using these fractal data (F-DL) and compared with an identical model trained on cardiac MRI data (CMR-DL). Both models were evaluated on prospectively acquired radial real-time cardiac MRI from 10 patients. Reconstructions were compared against compressed sensing(CS) and low-rank deep image prior (LR-DIP). All reconstrctuions were ranked for image quality, while ventricular volumes and ejection fraction were compared with reference breath-hold cine MRI. Results: There was no significant difference in qualitative ranking between F-DL and CMR-DL (p=0.9), while both outperformed CS and LR-DIP (p<0.001). Ventricular volumes and function derived from F-DL were similar to CMR-DL, showing no significant bias and accptable limits of agreement compared to reference cine imaging. However, LR-DIP had a signifcant bias (p=0.016) and wider lmits of agreement. Conclusion: DL models trained using synthetic fractal data can reconstruct real-time cardiac MRI with image quality and clinical measurements comparable to models trained on true cardiac MRI data. Fractal training data provide an open, scalable alternative to clinical datasets and may enable development of more generalisable DL reconstruction models for dynamic MRI.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Design and Development of an ML/DL Attack Resistance of RC-Based PUF for IoT Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28798)
*Joy Acharya,Smit Patel,Paawan Sharma,Mohendra Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RC的动态可重构PUF，使用32位挑战-响应对抵抗ML/DL建模攻击，实验显示多种ML模型在测试集上准确率接近随机猜测，证明其对抗建模攻击的强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网认证需要硬件安全方案，但传统PUF易受ML/DL建模攻击威胁。需要设计能抵抗此类攻击的PUF架构，同时保持资源受限环境下的低开销特性。

Method: 设计基于电阻-电容（RC）的动态可重构PUF，使用32位挑战-响应对。系统评估方法包括：生成CRP数据集并划分为训练、验证和测试集；使用多种ML技术（ANN、GBNN、DT、RF、XGBoost）训练PUF行为模型。

Result: 所有ML模型在训练集上达到100%准确率，但在测试集上表现接近随机猜测：ANN 51.05%、GBNN 53.27%、DT 50.06%、RF 52.08%、XGBoost 50.97%。这表明PUF对ML建模攻击具有强抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 提出的动态可重构RC-PUF能有效抵抗ML驱动的建模攻击，为物联网认证提供低成本、资源高效的硬件安全方案，确保设备验证可靠性而不影响计算效率或可扩展性。

Abstract: Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs) provide promising hardware security for IoT authentication, leveraging inherent randomness suitable for resource constrained environments. However, ML/DL modeling attacks threaten PUF security by learning challenge-response patterns. This work introduces a custom resistor-capacitor (RC) based dynamically reconfigurable PUF using 32-bit challenge-response pairs (CRPs) designed to resist such attacks. We systematically evaluated robustness by generating a CRP dataset and splitting it into training, validation, and test sets. Multiple ML techniques including Artificial Neural Networks (ANN), Gradient Boosted Neural Networks (GBNN), Decision Trees (DT), Random Forests (RF), and XGBoost, were trained to model PUF behavior. While all models achieved 100% training accuracy, test performance remained near random guessing: 51.05% (ANN), 53.27% (GBNN), 50.06% (DT), 52.08% (RF), and 50.97% (XGBoost). These results demonstrate the proposed PUF's strong resistance to ML-driven modeling attacks, as advanced algorithms fail to reproduce accurate responses. The dynamically reconfigurable architecture enhances robustness against adversarial threats with minimal resource overhead. This simple RC-PUF offers an effective, low-cost alternative to complex encryption for securing next-generation IoT authentication against machine learning-based threats, ensuring reliable device verification without compromising computational efficiency or scalability in deployed IoT networks.

</details>


### [122] [SafeClaw-R: Towards Safe and Secure Multi-Agent Personal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28807)
*Haoyu Wang,Zibo Xiao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeClaw-R是一个为LLM多智能体系统设计的运行时安全执行框架，通过在执行图上强制执行系统级安全不变量，在动作执行前进行仲裁，并系统性地为技能提供安全替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在提升个人生产力的同时带来了严重的安全风险：36.4%的OpenClaw内置技能存在高或关键风险，现有方法（静态护栏、LLM-as-a-Judge）在多智能体环境中缺乏可靠的实时执行和一致权威性。

Method: 提出SafeClaw-R框架，通过在执行图上强制执行系统级安全不变量，确保动作在执行前被仲裁，并系统性地为技能提供安全替代方案。框架在三个代表性领域进行评估：生产力平台、第三方技能生态系统和代码执行环境。

Result: 在Google Workspace场景中达到95.2%准确率（显著优于正则表达式基线的61.6%），检测到97.8%的恶意第三方技能模式，在对抗性代码执行基准测试中达到100%检测准确率。

Conclusion: SafeClaw-R能够为自主多智能体系统提供实用的运行时安全执行机制，有效解决LLM推理失败和提示注入攻击带来的安全风险。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MASs) are transforming personal productivity by autonomously executing complex, cross-platform tasks. Frameworks such as OpenClaw demonstrate the potential of locally deployed agents integrated with personal data and services, but this autonomy introduces significant safety and security risks. Unintended actions from LLM reasoning failures can cause irreversible harm, while prompt injection attacks may exfiltrate credentials or compromise the system. Our analysis shows that 36.4% of OpenClaw's built-in skills pose high or critical risks. Existing approaches, including static guardrails and LLM-as-a-Judge, lack reliable real-time enforcement and consistent authority in MAS settings. To address this, we propose SafeClaw-R, a framework that enforces safety as a system-level invariant over the execution graph by ensuring that actions are mediated prior to execution, and systematically augments skills with safe counterparts. We evaluate SafeClaw-R across three representative domains: productivity platforms, third-party skill ecosystems, and code execution environments. SafeClaw-R achieves 95.2% accuracy in Google Workspace scenarios, significantly outperforming regex baselines (61.6%), detects 97.8% of malicious third-party skill patterns, and achieves 100% detection accuracy in our adversarial code execution benchmark. These results demonstrate that SafeClaw-R enables practical runtime enforcement for autonomous MASs.

</details>


### [123] [SkillTester: Benchmarking Utility and Security of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28815)
*Leye Wang,Zixing Wang,Anjie Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillTester是一个评估智能体技能效用和安全性的工具，通过对比基准执行和带技能执行，结合安全探测套件，生成效用分数、安全分数和三级安全状态标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体优先的世界中，需要系统化的方法来评估智能体技能的实用性和安全性，确保技能既有效又安全。

Method: 采用配对基线执行和带技能执行条件的对比框架，结合独立的安全探测套件，基于比较效用原则和面向用户的简洁性原则，将原始执行结果标准化为量化分数和标签。

Result: 开发了SkillTester工具，提供公开服务（skilltester.ai）和开源项目（GitHub），能够生成技能效用分数、安全分数和三级安全状态标签。

Conclusion: SkillTester为智能体技能提供了一个比较性的质量保证框架，有助于在智能体优先的世界中评估和确保技能的实用性和安全性。

Abstract: This technical report presents SkillTester, a tool for evaluating the utility and security of agent skills. Its evaluation framework combines paired baseline and with-skill execution conditions with a separate security probe suite. Grounded in a comparative utility principle and a user-facing simplicity principle, the framework normalizes raw execution artifacts into a utility score, a security score, and a three-level security status label. More broadly, it can be understood as a comparative quality-assurance harness for agent skills in an agent-first world. The public service is deployed at https://skilltester.ai, and the broader project is maintained at https://github.com/skilltester-ai/skilltester.

</details>


### [124] [GUARD-SLM: Token Activation-Based Defense Against Jailbreak Attacks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28817)
*Md Jueal Mia,Joaquin Molto,Yanzhao Wu,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GUARD-SLM：一种基于令牌激活的轻量级方法，通过在表示空间中过滤恶意提示来保护小型语言模型免受越狱攻击，同时保持良性提示的处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLMs）作为大型语言模型（LLMs）的高效经济替代方案，在边缘设备部署中具有优势，但现有越狱防御对异构攻击的鲁棒性有限，主要因为对语言模型不同层内部表示如何促进越狱行为的理解不完整。

Method: 对9种越狱攻击在7个SLMs和3个LLMs上进行全面实证研究，分析不同层和模型架构的隐藏层激活，发现不同输入类型在内部表示空间中形成可区分的模式。基于此观察，提出GUARD-SLM——一种在表示空间中操作的轻量级令牌激活方法，在推理过程中过滤恶意提示同时保留良性提示。

Result: 分析显示SLMs对绕过安全对齐的恶意提示仍然高度脆弱。不同输入类型在内部表示空间中形成可区分的激活模式，GUARD-SLM方法能够有效过滤恶意提示。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了语言模型各层的鲁棒性限制，为安全部署小型语言模型提供了实用方向。GUARD-SLM方法通过利用内部表示空间的模式识别能力，为SLMs提供了有效的轻量级安全防护方案。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are emerging as efficient and economically viable alternatives to Large Language Models (LLMs), offering competitive performance with significantly lower computational costs and latency. These advantages make SLMs suitable for resource-constrained and efficient deployment on edge devices. However, existing jailbreak defenses show limited robustness against heterogeneous attacks, largely due to an incomplete understanding of the internal representations across different layers of language models that facilitate jailbreak behaviors. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study on 9 jailbreak attacks across 7 SLMs and 3 LLMs. Our analysis shows that SLMs remain highly vulnerable to malicious prompts that bypass safety alignment. We analyze hidden-layer activations across different layers and model architectures, revealing that different input types form distinguishable patterns in the internal representation space. Based on this observation, we propose GUARD-SLM, a lightweight token activation-based method that operates in the representation space to filter malicious prompts during inference while preserving benign ones. Our findings highlight robustness limitations across layers of language models and provide a practical direction for secure small language model deployment.

</details>


### [125] [SNEAKDOOR: Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against Distribution Matching-based Dataset Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28824)
*He Yang,Dongyi Lv,Song Ma,Wei Xi,Jizhong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sneakdoor是一种针对数据集压缩的隐蔽后门攻击方法，通过利用类别决策边界漏洞和生成输入感知触发器来提升隐蔽性，同时保持攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集压缩后门攻击方法在平衡攻击成功率和干净测试准确率方面取得进展，但在隐蔽性方面不足，特别是在隐藏压缩数据的视觉伪影或推理时引入的扰动方面存在缺陷。

Method: Sneakdoor利用类别决策边界的固有漏洞，并引入生成模块构建与局部特征几何对齐的输入感知触发器，从而最小化可检测性。这种联合设计使攻击对人类检查和统计检测都难以察觉。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，Sneakdoor在攻击成功率、干净测试准确率和隐蔽性之间实现了令人信服的平衡，显著提高了合成数据和触发样本的不可见性，同时保持高攻击效果。

Conclusion: Sneakdoor通过增强隐蔽性而不损害攻击效果，解决了数据集压缩后门攻击中的隐蔽性挑战，为这一领域提供了更隐蔽的攻击方法。

Abstract: Dataset condensation aims to synthesize compact yet informative datasets that retain the training efficacy of full-scale data, offering substantial gains in efficiency. Recent studies reveal that the condensation process can be vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where malicious triggers are injected into the condensation dataset, manipulating model behavior during inference. While prior approaches have made progress in balancing attack success rate and clean test accuracy, they often fall short in preserving stealthiness, especially in concealing the visual artifacts of condensed data or the perturbations introduced during inference. To address this challenge, we introduce Sneakdoor, which enhances stealthiness without compromising attack effectiveness. Sneakdoor exploits the inherent vulnerability of class decision boundaries and incorporates a generative module that constructs input-aware triggers aligned with local feature geometry, thereby minimizing detectability. This joint design enables the attack to remain imperceptible to both human inspection and statistical detection. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that Sneakdoor achieves a compelling balance among attack success rate, clean test accuracy, and stealthiness, substantially improving the invisibility of both the synthetic data and triggered samples while maintaining high attack efficacy. The code is available at https://github.com/XJTU-AI-Lab/SneakDoor.

</details>


### [126] [Differential Privacy for Symbolic Trajectories via the Permute-and-Flip Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28903)
*Alexander Benvenuti,Huaiyuan Rao,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种用于符号系统状态轨迹的差分隐私机制，通过随机选择低误差私有近似词替代原始敏感词，避免指数级枚举，在真实交通数据上误差减少55%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私技术主要针对数值数据，而符号系统（如马尔可夫链、有限状态自动机）的状态轨迹无法使用典型的噪声添加技术，需要开发适用于非数值数据的差分隐私机制

Method: 基于permute-and-flip机制，开发新的私有词生成方法，避免枚举指数级大的词列表，通过随机选择低误差私有近似词来保护符号轨迹

Result: 理论上证明机制精度不低于现有最优方法，实证在真实交通数据集上，相比传统隐私实现减少高达55%的误差

Conclusion: 成功开发了适用于符号系统状态轨迹的差分隐私机制，解决了非数值数据的隐私保护问题，在保持隐私的同时显著降低了误差

Abstract: Privacy techniques have been developed for data-driven systems, but systems with non-numeric data cannot use typical noise-adding techniques. Therefore, we develop a new mechanism for privatizing state trajectories of symbolic systems that may be represented as words over a finite alphabet. Such systems include Markov chains, Markov decision processes, and finite-state automata, and we protect their symbolic trajectories with differential privacy. The mechanism we develop randomly selects a private approximation to be released in place of the original sensitive word, with a bias towards low-error private words. This work is based on the permute-and-flip mechanism for differential privacy, which can be applied to non-numeric data. However, a naïve implementation would have to enumerate an exponentially large list of words to generate a private word. As a result, we develop a new mechanism that generates private words without ever needing to enumerate such a list. We prove that the accuracy of our mechanism is never worse than the prior state of the art, and we empirically show on a real traffic dataset that it introduces up to $55\%$ less error than the prior state of the art under a conventional privacy implementation.

</details>


### [127] [Privacy Guard & Token Parsimony by Prompt and Context Handling and LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28972)
*Alessio Langiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出本地"隐私守卫"框架，通过小型语言模型进行抽象摘要和自动提示优化，将高风险查询重定向到零信任模型，实现零泄漏和运营成本降低的双重目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模采用大型语言模型需要在运营成本和数据隐私之间权衡。当前路由框架降低成本但忽略提示敏感性，使用户和机构面临向第三方云提供商泄露的风险。

Method: 提出本地"隐私守卫"框架，由本地小型语言模型驱动，执行抽象摘要和自动提示优化，将提示分解为聚焦子任务，将高风险查询重定向到零信任或NDA覆盖模型。采用LIFO上下文压缩机制限制工作内存。

Result: 在1000样本数据集上验证，实现45%混合运营成本降低，个人秘密100%编辑成功，通过LLM-as-a-Judge评估显示APO压缩响应比原始基线获得85%偏好率。

Conclusion: 令牌节约和零泄漏是同一上下文压缩算子的数学对偶投影，高级上下文管理与隐私管理本质上是一致的。

Abstract: The large-scale adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) forces a trade-off between operational cost (OpEx) and data privacy. Current routing frameworks reduce costs but ignore prompt sensitivity, exposing users and institutions to leakage risks towards third-party cloud providers. We formalise the "Inseparability Paradigm": advanced context management intrinsically coincides with privacy management. We propose a local "Privacy Guard" -- a holistic contextual observer powered by an on-premise Small Language Model (SLM) -- that performs abstractive summarisation and Automatic Prompt Optimisation (APO) to decompose prompts into focused sub-tasks, re-routing high-risk queries to Zero-Trust or NDA-covered models. This dual mechanism simultaneously eliminates sensitive inference vectors (Zero Leakage) and reduces cloud token payloads (OpEx Reduction). A LIFO-based context compacting mechanism further bounds working memory, limiting the emergent leakage surface. We validate the framework through a 2x2 benchmark (Lazy vs. Expert users; Personal vs. Institutional secrets) on a 1,000-sample dataset, achieving a 45% blended OpEx reduction, 100% redaction success on personal secrets, and -- via LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation -- an 85% preference rate for APO-compressed responses over raw baselines. Our results demonstrate that Token Parsimony and Zero Leakage are mathematically dual projections of the same contextual compression operator.

</details>


### [128] [Attesting LLM Pipelines: Enforcing Verifiable Training and Release Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28988)
*Zhuoran Tan,Jeremy Singer,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于认证的推广门控机制，通过密码学绑定训练和发布声明与模型工件，在进入可信环境前验证声明证据、执行安全策略，以应对LLM供应链风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型语言模型系统从第三方组件（预训练权重、微调适配器、数据集等）快速组装，这种速度带来了供应链风险，包括依赖项被篡改、恶意中心工件、不安全反序列化、伪造来源和后门模型。核心问题是训练和发布声明很少与所描述的工件进行密码学绑定，导致跨团队和阶段的执行不一致。

Method: 提出认证感知的推广门控机制：在工件进入可信环境（训练、微调、部署）之前，门控验证声明证据，强制执行安全加载和静态扫描策略，并应用默认安全部署约束。组织运行运行时安全工具时，门控可通过插件摄取标准化动态信号以减少高风险工件的不确定性。

Result: 提出了实用的声明到控制映射和评估蓝图，使用代表性供应链场景和操作指标（覆盖范围和决策），为完整研究论文规划了路径。

Conclusion: 认证感知推广门控机制通过密码学绑定声明与工件，在进入可信环境前进行系统化验证和执行安全策略，能够有效应对LLM供应链安全风险，提高整个开发部署流程的安全性。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Model (LLM) systems are assembled from third-party artifacts such as pre-trained weights, fine-tuning adapters, datasets, dependency packages, and container images, fetched through automated pipelines. This speed comes with supply-chain risks, including compromised dependencies, malicious hub artifacts, unsafe deserialization, forged provenance, and backdoored models. A core gap is that training and release claims (e.g., data and code lineage, build environment, and security scanning results) are rarely cryptographically bound to the artifacts they describe, making enforcement inconsistent across teams and stages. We propose an attestation-aware promotion gate: before an artifact is admitted into trusted environments (training, fine-tuning, deployment), the gate verifies claim evidence, enforces safe loading and static scanning policies, and applies secure-by-default deployment constraints. When organizations operate runtime security tooling, the same gate can optionally ingest standardized dynamic signals via plugins to reduce uncertainty for high-risk artifacts. We outline a practical claims-to-controls mapping and an evaluation blueprint using representative supply-chain scenarios and operational metrics (coverage and decisions), charting a path toward a full research paper.

</details>


### [129] [Trojan-Speak: Bypassing Constitutional Classifiers with No Jailbreak Tax via Adversarial Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29038)
*Bilgehan Sel,Xuanli He,Alwin Peng,Ming Jin,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Trojan-Speak是一种对抗性微调方法，通过课程学习和GRPO混合强化学习教模型学习规避LLM内容分类器的通信协议，在保持模型能力的同时实现99%以上的分类器规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI提供商提供的微调API创造了新的攻击面，攻击者可以通过针对性微调绕过安全措施。现有对抗性微调方法会导致超过25%的推理能力退化，需要开发既能规避分类器又能保持模型性能的方法。

Method: Trojan-Speak采用课程学习结合GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的混合强化学习方法，教导模型学习一种能够规避基于LLM的内容分类器的通信协议。该方法在保持模型能力的同时实现分类器规避。

Result: Trojan-Speak仅导致不到5%的推理基准性能退化（相比之前方法的25%以上），对14B+参数模型实现99%以上的分类器规避。微调后的模型能够为Anthropic宪法分类器漏洞赏金计划中的专家级CBRN查询提供详细响应。

Conclusion: 当攻击者拥有微调访问权限时，仅依靠基于LLM的内容分类器不足以防止危险信息泄露。激活级探针可以显著提高对此类攻击的鲁棒性，表明需要更强大的安全机制来应对对抗性微调威胁。

Abstract: Fine-tuning APIs offered by major AI providers create new attack surfaces where adversaries can bypass safety measures through targeted fine-tuning. We introduce Trojan-Speak, an adversarial fine-tuning method that bypasses Anthropic's Constitutional Classifiers. Our approach uses curriculum learning combined with GRPO-based hybrid reinforcement learning to teach models a communication protocol that evades LLM-based content classification. Crucially, while prior adversarial fine-tuning approaches report more than 25% capability degradation on reasoning benchmarks, Trojan-Speak incurs less than 5% degradation while achieving 99+% classifier evasion for models with 14B+ parameters. We demonstrate that fine-tuned models can provide detailed responses to expert-level CBRN (Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear) queries from Anthropic's Constitutional Classifiers bug-bounty program. Our findings reveal that LLM-based content classifiers alone are insufficient for preventing dangerous information disclosure when adversaries have fine-tuning access, and we show that activation-level probes can substantially improve robustness to such attacks.

</details>


### [130] [Uncovering Relationships between Android Developers, User Privacy, and Developer Willingness to Reduce Fingerprinting Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29063)
*Alex Berke,Güliz Seray Tuncay,Michael Specter,Mihai Christodorescu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究调查了Android开发者对移动设备指纹识别隐私保护的态度，发现大多数开发者支持平台引入反指纹识别功能，即使这会增加开发工作量，但更希望这些功能是可选的而非强制的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Android和iOS平台已采取措施限制用户跟踪以保护隐私，但应用仍通过设备指纹识别进行隐蔽跟踪。研究旨在了解开发者对平台隐私保护措施的看法，以及他们愿意为保护用户免受指纹识别而做出改变的意愿。

Method: 通过对246名Android开发者进行问卷调查，向他们展示一个假设的Android平台变更：该变更以增加开发者工作量为代价来保护用户免受指纹识别。研究评估了开发者对这一变更的支持程度、偏好（可选vs强制），以及他们对隐私与工作量权衡的考虑。

Result: 1. 89%的开发者支持这一变更，即使他们预期会付出显著工作量；2. 开发者更倾向于变更是可选的而非强制的；3. 使用指纹识别的开发者支持变更的可能性是其他开发者的6倍，尽管他们受影响最大；4. 开发者最关心合规性和执行问题；5. 大多数开发者认为iOS在保护用户隐私方面优于Android，但非常熟悉指纹识别的开发者中这一差异显著减小。

Conclusion: 平台和开发者之间存在重要的合作机会来共同构建隐私保护机制。研究提出了平台可以采取的具体行动来促进这种合作，包括将隐私保护功能设计为可选而非强制，并解决开发者的合规性和执行顾虑。

Abstract: The major mobile platforms, Android and iOS, have introduced changes that restrict user tracking to improve user privacy, yet apps continue to covertly track users via device fingerprinting. We study the opportunity to improve this dynamic with a case study on mobile fingerprinting that evaluates developers' perceptions of how well platforms protect user privacy and how developers perceive platform privacy interventions. Specifically, we study developers' willingness to make changes to protect users from fingerprinting and how developers consider trade-offs between user privacy and developer effort. We do this via a survey of 246 Android developers, presented with a hypothetical Android change that protects users from fingerprinting at the cost of additional developer effort.
  We find developers overwhelmingly (89%) support this change, even when they anticipate significant effort, yet prefer the change be optional versus required. Surprisingly, developers who use fingerprinting are six times more likely to support the change, despite being most impacted by it. We also find developers are most concerned about compliance and enforcement. In addition, our results show that while most rank iOS above Android for protecting user privacy, this distinction significantly reduces among developers very familiar with fingerprinting. Thus there is an important opportunity for platforms and developers to collaboratively build privacy protections, and we present actionable ways platforms can facilitate this.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Corner Patches: Semantics-Aware Backdoor Attack in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29328)
*Kavindu Herath,Joshua Zhao,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SABLE：一种在联邦学习中使用的语义感知后门攻击方法，使用自然、内容一致的语义触发器（如太阳镜等属性变化），通过特征分离和参数正则化优化恶意目标，在保持良性准确率的同时实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习后门攻击研究多使用合成角块或分布外模式等不现实的触发器，需要在实际场景中评估语义有意义、分布内且视觉合理的后门威胁。

Method: 提出SABLE方法：1）构建自然、内容一致的语义触发器（如语义属性变化）；2）通过特征分离和参数正则化优化聚合感知的恶意目标，使攻击者更新接近良性更新；3）在CelebA和GTSRB数据集上实例化，仅毒化恶意客户端本地数据的可解释子集。

Result: 在异构客户端分区和多种聚合规则（FedAvg、Trimmed Mean、MultiKrum、FLAME）下，语义驱动的触发器在保持良性测试准确率的同时实现了高目标攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 语义对齐的后门在联邦学习中仍然是强大且实际的威胁，仅基于合成块触发器的鲁棒性声明可能过于乐观。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on federated learning (FL) are most often evaluated with synthetic corner patches or out-of-distribution (OOD) patterns that are unlikely to arise in practice. In this paper, we revisit the backdoor threat to standard FL (a single global model) under a more realistic setting where triggers must be semantically meaningful, in-distribution, and visually plausible. We propose SABLE, a Semantics-Aware Backdoor for LEarning in federated settings, which constructs natural, content-consistent triggers (e.g., semantic attribute changes such as sunglasses) and optimizes an aggregation-aware malicious objective with feature separation and parameter regularization to keep attacker updates close to benign ones. We instantiate SABLE on CelebA hair-color classification and the German Traffic Sign Recognition Benchmark (GTSRB), poisoning only a small, interpretable subset of each malicious client's local data while otherwise following the standard FL protocol. Across heterogeneous client partitions and multiple aggregation rules (FedAvg, Trimmed Mean, MultiKrum, and FLAME), our semantics-driven triggers achieve high targeted attack success rates while preserving benign test accuracy. These results show that semantics-aligned backdoors remain a potent and practical threat in federated learning, and that robustness claims based solely on synthetic patch triggers can be overly optimistic.

</details>


### [132] [Deep Learning-Assisted Improved Differential Fault Attacks on Lightweight Stream Ciphers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29382)
*Kok Ping Lim,Dongyang Jia,Iftekhar Salam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将深度学习辅助的差分故障攻击应用于三种轻量级流密码（ACORNv3、MORUSv2和ATOM），在未知位置的单比特翻转故障模型下，通过MLP模型识别故障位置，显著提升了攻击效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备广泛部署轻量级密码原语，这些设备易受物理攻击特别是故障攻击。深度学习在密码分析中展现出潜力，但在流密码故障攻击中的应用有限，尤其是在宽松故障模型下。

Method: 使用多层感知器（MLP）模型识别未知位置的单比特翻转故障位置，并引入基于阈值的方法优化秘密恢复所需的故障注入次数。对ACORNv3、MORUSv2和ATOM三种流密码进行实验验证。

Result: MLP模型在故障位置识别上取得高准确率：ACORNv3 0.999880、MORUSv2 0.999231、ATOM 0.823568。秘密恢复方面，ACORNv3需要21-34次故障注入，MORUSv2需要213-248次故障注入（最多6位猜测），攻击复杂度均低于现有工作。ATOM显示出更高的安全裕度。

Conclusion: 深度学习辅助的差分故障攻击对轻量级流密码有效，显著降低了攻击复杂度。ACORNv3和MORUSv2相对脆弱，而ATOM在宽松故障模型下具有更强的安全性。这是首次对ATOM进行差分故障攻击的实验研究。

Abstract: Lightweight cryptographic primitives are widely deployed in resource-constraint environment, particularly in the Internet of Things (IoT) devices. Due to their public accessibility, these devices are vulnerable to physical attacks, especially fault attacks. Recently, deep learning-based cryptanalytic techniques have demonstrated promising results; however, their application to fault attacks remains limited, particularly for stream ciphers. In this work, we investigate the feasibility of deep learning assisted differential fault attack on three lightweight stream ciphers, namely ACORNv3, MORUSv2 and ATOM, under a relaxed fault model, where a single-bit bit-flipping fault is injected at an unknown location. We train multilayer perceptron (MLP) models to identify the fault locations. Experimental results show that the trained models achieve high identification accuracies of 0.999880, 0.999231 and 0.823568 for ACORNv3, MORUSv2 and ATOM, respectively, and outperform traditional signature-based methods. For the secret recovery process, we introduce a threshold-based method to optimize the number of fault injections required to recover the secret information. The results show that the initial state of ACORN can be recovered with 21 to 34 faults; while MORUS requires 213 to 248 faults, with at most 6 bits of guessing. Both attacks reduce the attack complexity compared to existing works. For ATOM, the results show that it possesses a higher security margin, as majority of state bits in the Non-linear Feedback Shift Register (NFSR) can only be recovered under a precise control model. To the best of our knowledge, this work provides the first experimental results of differential fault attacks on ATOM.

</details>


### [133] [Security in LLM-as-a-Judge: A Comprehensive SoK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29403)
*Aiman Almasoud,Antony Anju,Marco Arazzi,Mert Cihangiroglu,Vignesh Kumar Kembu,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P.,Saraga Sakthidharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统化研究了LLM-as-a-Judge（LaaJ）范式的安全方面，通过分析863篇文献中的45项相关研究，提出了基于LaaJ在安全生态中角色的分类法，揭示了LLM评估框架的重大漏洞和新兴威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽然提高了评估的可扩展性和效率，但引入了新的安全风险和可靠性问题，这些风险尚未得到充分探索。LLM评估者既可能成为对抗性操纵的目标，也可能成为攻击的工具，从而危及评估管道的可信度。

Method: 对主要学术数据库进行全面的文献综述，分析863篇文献并筛选出2020-2026年间发表的45项相关研究。基于此研究提出了一个分类法，根据LaaJ在安全生态中的角色组织近期研究，区分针对LaaJ系统的攻击、通过LaaJ进行的攻击、利用LaaJ进行安全防御的应用，以及LaaJ作为安全相关领域评估策略的应用。

Result: 研究揭示了LLM评估框架存在重大漏洞，包括对抗性操纵、可靠性问题等。同时识别了当前方法的局限性、新兴威胁和开放研究挑战，并指出了提高LLM评估系统鲁棒性和可靠性的有前景方向。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统化地研究了LLM-as-a-Judge范式的安全方面，提出了分类法并进行了比较分析。研究结果强调了LLM评估框架的安全脆弱性，并为开发更安全、更可信的LLM-as-a-Judge系统指明了关键研究方向。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge (LaaJ) is a novel paradigm in which powerful language models are used to assess the quality, safety, or correctness of generated outputs. While this paradigm has significantly improved the scalability and efficiency of evaluation processes, it also introduces novel security risks and reliability concerns that remain largely unexplored. In particular, LLM-based judges can become both targets of adversarial manipulation and instruments through which attacks are conducted, potentially compromising the trustworthiness of evaluation pipelines. In this paper, we present the first Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) focusing on the security aspects of LLM-as-a-Judge systems. We perform a comprehensive literature review across major academic databases, analyzing 863 works and selecting 45 relevant studies published between 2020 and 2026. Based on this study, we propose a taxonomy that organizes recent research according to the role played by LLM-as-a-Judge in the security landscape, distinguishing between attacks targeting LaaJ systems, attacks performed through LaaJ, defenses leveraging LaaJ for security purposes, and applications where LaaJ is used as an evaluation strategy in security-related domains. We further provide a comparative analysis of existing approaches, highlighting current limitations, emerging threats, and open research challenges. Our findings reveal significant vulnerabilities in LLM-based evaluation frameworks, as well as promising directions for improving their robustness and reliability. Finally, we outline key research opportunities that can guide the development of more secure and trustworthy LLM-as-a-Judge systems.

</details>


### [134] [TrafficMoE: Heterogeneity-aware Mixture of Experts for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29520)
*Qing He,Xiaowei Fu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrafficMoE提出了一种用于加密流量分类的Disentangle-Filter-Aggregate框架，通过双分支稀疏MoE分离头部和载荷，引入不确定性感知过滤机制，并采用路由引导的动态特征聚合，显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密流量分类方法采用静态同质化处理管道，将结构化头部和随机化加密载荷统一处理，导致原始协议信号与随机加密噪声纠缠，降低了细粒度判别特征的质量。

Method: 提出Disentangle-Filter-Aggregate范式：1) 使用双分支稀疏Mixture-of-Experts分离头部和载荷进行模态特定建模；2) 引入不确定性感知过滤机制量化表示可靠性并选择性抑制高方差表示；3) 采用路由引导策略动态聚合跨模态特征，根据流量上下文自适应加权贡献。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，TrafficMoE持续优于最先进方法，验证了在加密流量分析中异构感知建模的必要性。

Conclusion: TrafficMoE通过DFA范式突破了静态建模瓶颈，通过分离-过滤-聚合策略最大化表示效率，专注于最具判别性的流量特征，为加密流量分类提供了有效的异构感知建模框架。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification is a critical task for network security. While deep learning has advanced this field, the occlusion of payload semantics by encryption severely challenges standard modeling approaches. Most existing frameworks rely on static and homogeneous pipelines that apply uniform parameter sharing and static fusion strategies across all inputs. This one-size-fits-all static design is inherently flawed: by forcing structured headers and randomized payloads into a unified processing pipeline, it inevitably entangles the raw protocol signals with stochastic encryption noise, thereby degrading the fine-grained discriminative features. In this paper, we propose TrafficMoE, a framework that breaks through the bottleneck of static modeling by establishing a Disentangle-Filter-Aggregate (DFA) paradigm. Specifically, to resolve the structural between-components conflict, the architecture disentangles headers and payloads using dual-branch sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), enabling modality-specific modeling. To mitigate the impact of stochastic noise, an uncertainty-aware filtering mechanism is introduced to quantify reliability and selectively suppress high-variance representations. Finally, to overcome the limitations of static fusion, a routing-guided strategy aggregates cross-modality features dynamically, that adaptively weighs contributions based on traffic context. With this DFA paradigm, TrafficMoE maximizes representational efficiency by focusing solely on the most discriminative traffic features. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate TrafficMoE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, validating the necessity of heterogeneity-aware modeling in encrypted traffic analysis. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Posuly/TrafficMoE_main.

</details>


### [135] [Mean Masked Autoencoder with Flow-Mixing for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29537)
*Xiao Liu,Xiaowei Fu,Fuxiang Huang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MMAE：基于教师-学生MAE范式与流混合策略的加密流量预训练模型，通过自蒸馏机制和多粒度理解提升流量分类性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MAE的流量分类方法局限于单个流量的字节级重建，缺乏对流量中多粒度上下文关系的感知，存在信息瓶颈

Method: 提出MMAE框架：1）教师-学生自蒸馏机制，教师提供未掩码的流级语义监督；2）动态流混合策略替代随机掩码，构建跨流混合样本；3）包重要性感知掩码预测器，利用包级侧信道统计动态掩码高语义密度token

Result: 在多个数据集（加密应用、恶意软件、攻击流量）上的实验表明，MMAE实现了最先进的性能

Conclusion: MMAE通过教师-学生交互、流混合策略和包感知掩码机制，有效解决了现有MAE方法在多粒度上下文感知方面的局限性，显著提升了加密流量分类性能

Abstract: Network traffic classification using self-supervised pre-training models based on Masked Autoencoders (MAE) has demonstrated a huge potential. However, existing methods are confined to isolated byte-level reconstruction of individual flows, lacking adequate perception of the multi-granularity contextual relationship in traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Mean MAE (MMAE), a teacher-student MAE paradigm with flow mixing strategy for building encrypted traffic pre-training model. MMAE employs a self-distillation mechanism for teacher-student interaction, where the teacher provides unmasked flow-level semantic supervision to advance the student from local byte reconstruction to multi-granularity comprehension. To break the information bottleneck in individual flows, we introduce a dynamic Flow Mixing (FlowMix) strategy to replace traditional random masking mechanism. By constructing challenging cross-flow mixed samples with interferences, it compels the model to learn discriminative representations from distorted tokens. Furthermore, we design a Packet-importance aware Mask Predictor (PMP) equipped with an attention bias mechanism that leverages packet-level side-channel statistics to dynamically mask tokens with high semantic density. Numerous experiments on a number of datasets covering encrypted applications, malware, and attack traffic demonstrate that MMAE achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code is available at https://github.com/lx6c78/MMAE

</details>


### [136] [HPCCFA: Leveraging Hardware Performance Counters for Control Flow Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29749)
*Claudius Pott,Luca Wilke,Jan Wichelmann,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HPCCFA方法，利用硬件性能计数器在商用CPU上实现控制流认证，扩展TEE的安全边界以检测运行时攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信执行环境（TEE）依赖静态认证机制，无法防护运行时攻击。控制流认证（CFA）能检测此类攻击，但需要硬件支持。本研究旨在探索如何在商用CPU上实现硬件支持的控制流认证

Method: 提出HPCCFA机制，利用硬件性能计数器（HPC）实现控制流认证。在RISC-V平台的Keystone TEE上构建概念验证实现，研究测量点数量与运行时保护的相互作用

Result: 成功在Keystone/RISC-V上实现HPCCFA原型。评估显示检测可靠性与性能开销之间存在权衡关系，测量点数量影响检测效果和系统性能

Conclusion: HPCCFA证明了在商用CPU上利用硬件性能计数器实现控制流认证的可行性，为TEE提供了运行时攻击防护能力，但需要在检测可靠性和性能开销之间进行平衡

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) allow the secure execution of code on remote systems without the need to trust their operators. They use static attestation as a central mechanism for establishing trust, allowing remote parties to verify that their code is executed unmodified in an isolated environment. However, this form of attestation does not cover runtime attacks, where an attacker exploits vulnerabilities in the software inside the TEE. Control Flow Attestation (CFA), a form of runtime attestation, is designed to detect such attacks.
  In this work, we present a method to extend TEEs with CFA and discuss how it can prevent exploitation in the event of detected control flow violations. Furthermore, we introduce HPCCFA, a mechanism that uses HPCs for CFA purposes, enabling hardware-backed trace generation on commodity CPUs. We demonstrate the feasibility of HPCCFA on a proof-of-concept implementation for Keystone on RISC-V. Our evaluation investigates the interplay of the number of measurement points and runtime protection, and reveals a trade-off between detection reliability and performance overhead.

</details>


### [137] [Architecting Secure AI Agents: Perspectives on System-Level Defenses Against Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30016)
*Chong Xiang,Drew Zagieboylo,Shaona Ghosh,Sanjay Kariyappa,Kai Greshake,Hanshen Xiao,Chaowei Xiao,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该立场论文提出针对LLM智能体间接提示注入攻击的系统级防御框架，强调动态重规划、受限模型决策和个性化人机交互三大核心原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的AI智能体容易受到间接提示注入攻击，恶意指令可通过非受信数据触发危险行为。现有防御措施不足，需要系统级解决方案来保障智能体安全性。

Method: 提出三个核心立场：1）动态任务和现实环境中需要动态重规划和安全策略更新；2）上下文相关安全决策仍需LLM参与，但需严格限制模型的观察和决策范围；3）固有模糊情况下应将个性化和人机交互作为核心设计考量。同时强调系统级防御作为智能体系统骨架的重要性。

Result: 构建了系统级防御的理论框架，指出现有基准测试的局限性可能造成虚假的安全感，强调系统级防御能够结构化控制智能体行为、整合规则和模型安全检查，并为模型鲁棒性和人机交互研究提供更精准的方向。

Conclusion: 应对间接提示注入攻击需要系统级防御方法，通过动态重规划、受限模型决策和个性化人机交互三大原则构建安全智能体系统，同时需要改进评估基准并重视系统级防御在智能体架构中的骨架作用。

Abstract: AI agents, predominantly powered by large language models (LLMs), are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection, in which malicious instructions embedded in untrusted data can trigger dangerous agent actions. This position paper discusses our vision for system-level defenses against indirect prompt injection attacks. We articulate three positions: (1) dynamic replanning and security policy updates are often necessary for dynamic tasks and realistic environments; (2) certain context-dependent security decisions would still require LLMs (or other learned models), but should only be made within system designs that strictly constrain what the model can observe and decide; (3) in inherently ambiguous cases, personalization and human interaction should be treated as core design considerations. In addition to our main positions, we discuss limitations of existing benchmarks that can create a false sense of utility and security. We also highlight the value of system-level defenses, which serve as the skeleton of agentic systems by structuring and controlling agent behaviors, integrating rule-based and model-based security checks, and enabling more targeted research on model robustness and human interaction.

</details>


### [138] [EnsembleSHAP: Faithful and Certifiably Robust Attribution for Random Subspace Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30034)
*Yanting Wang,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出EnsembleSHAP方法，为随机子空间方法提供高效、安全且具有理论保证的特征归因解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机子空间方法在安全应用中广泛使用，但缺乏充分的解释性分析。现有特征归因方法（如Shapley值、LIME）在应用于随机子空间方法时存在计算效率低和安全性不足的问题

Method: 提出EnsembleSHAP方法，通过重用随机子空间方法的计算副产品，实现高效、安全且具有理论保证的特征归因。该方法具有计算效率高、保持局部准确性等特性，并提供针对隐私保护攻击的保证防护

Result: 该方法在计算效率、特征归因有效性（局部准确性等）和安全性方面表现优异。首次建立了针对解释保持攻击的可证明鲁棒性，并通过对抗攻击、后门攻击和越狱攻击等实证评估验证了其有效性

Conclusion: EnsembleSHAP为随机子空间方法提供了首个高效、安全且具有理论保证的特征归因解决方案，填补了该领域在解释性和安全性方面的空白

Abstract: Random subspace method has wide security applications such as providing certified defenses against adversarial and backdoor attacks, and building robustly aligned LLM against jailbreaking attacks. However, the explanation of random subspace method lacks sufficient exploration. Existing state-of-the-art feature attribution methods, such as Shapley value and LIME, are computationally impractical and lacks security guarantee when applied to random subspace method. In this work, we propose EnsembleSHAP, an intrinsically faithful and secure feature attribution for random subspace method that reuses its computational byproducts. Specifically, our feature attribution method is 1) computationally efficient, 2) maintains essential properties of effective feature attribution (such as local accuracy), and 3) offers guaranteed protection against privacy-preserving attacks on feature attribution methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to establish provable robustness against explanation-preserving attacks. We also perform comprehensive evaluations for our explanation's effectiveness when faced with different empirical attacks, including backdoor attacks, adversarial attacks, and jailbreak attacks. The code is at https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/EnsembleSHAP. WARNING: This document may include content that could be considered harmful.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Linear Regression from 1-bit Quantized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28989)
*Daniel Hill,Martin Slawski*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究单比特抖动量化下的线性回归，提出基于二次规划最小二乘问题的估计器，分析其非渐近误差界和渐近分布，并扩展到高维预测变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据丰富但存储/传输资源稀缺的环境普遍存在，需要研究在预测变量、其平方项和响应变量都经过单比特抖动量化情况下的线性回归问题。

Method: 提出基于二次规划最小二乘问题中二次项和线性项的插件估计的估计器。通过二次规划公式化最小二乘问题，使用插件方法估计其中的二次项和线性项。

Result: 提供了该估计器的ℓ₂-估计误差的非渐近界，并在预测变量数量固定时获得了其渐近分布，可用于推断和相对于普通最小二乘估计器的均方误差效率分析。表明在所考虑的量化协议下，无法期望对该估计器有实质性改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计器在单比特抖动量化环境下是有效的，可以扩展到包含草图化和量化的压缩管道，并适用于高维预测变量。数值实验验证了理论发现。

Abstract: Motivated by the prevalence of environments in which data is abundant while resources for storage and/or transmission might be scarce, we study linear regression when predictors, their squares, and responses are subject to single-bit dithered quantization. An estimator relying on plug-in estimation of the quadratic and linear terms in the quadratic program formulation of the least squares problem is proposed. We provide a non-asymptotic bound on the $\ell_2$-estimation error of this estimator and obtain its asymptotic distribution when the number of predictors is fixed, which can be used for inference and an investigation of the mean-square error efficiency relative to the ordinary least squares estimator. It is shown that for the quantization protocol under consideration, substantial improvements over the proposed estimator cannot be expected. A compression pipeline in which the underlying data is first subject to sketching and subsequently quantization can be studied within our framework as well. We also present an extension to address high-dimensional predictors. Numerical experiments with synthetic data complement our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [140] [Computable error bounds for high-dimensional Edgeworth expansions in sphericity testing under two-step monotone incomplete data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29120)
*Tetsuya Sato,Tomoyuki Nakagawa*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对高维两步单调不完整数据下的单样本球性检验，推导了似然比检验统计量的Edgeworth展开，并获得了可计算的误差界，相比现有渐近展开具有更好的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维两步单调不完整数据下，现有似然比检验统计量和修正似然比检验统计量的零分布渐近展开在精度上存在不足，需要更准确的近似方法。

Method: 推导了似然比检验统计量零分布的Edgeworth展开，并获得了可计算的误差界；通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行数值实验，评估标准化检验统计量分布函数与Edgeworth展开之间的最大绝对误差。

Result: 提出的Edgeworth展开相比现有渐近展开具有更好的近似精度；数值实验验证了Edgeworth展开的准确性以及可计算误差界的性能。

Conclusion: 对于高维两步单调不完整数据下的单样本球性检验问题，Edgeworth展开提供了比传统渐近展开更精确的零分布近似，并可通过可计算误差界量化近似精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the sphericity test for a one-sample problem under high-dimensional two-step monotone incomplete data. Existing asymptotic expansions for the null distributions of the likelihood ratio test (LRT) statistic and modified LRT statistic are inaccurate in high-dimensional settings. Therefore, we derive Edgeworth expansions for the null distribution of the LRT statistic in such settings and obtain computable error bounds. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our proposed Edgeworth expansions provide better approximation accuracy than the existing asymptotic expansions. We also conduct numerical experiments using Monte Carlo simulations to evaluate the maximum absolute error (MAE) between the distribution function of the standardized test statistic and Edgeworth expansions for the null distribution of the LRT statistic, as well as to assess the performance of the computable error bounds.

</details>


### [141] [Concentration of the bootstrap empirical process, with applications to statistical inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29415)
*Guillaume Maillard,Adrien Saumard*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于可交换权重的自助法框架下，自助经验过程上确界的集中不等式，包括条件于数据的集中性以及条件期望的集中性，并应用于均值向量置信区域和两样本置换检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自助经验过程的集中性对于理解自助法的统计性质至关重要。现有研究主要关注自助法的渐近性质，但非渐近的集中不等式对于实际应用中的置信区间构建和假设检验具有重要意义。特别是对于可交换权重的自助法框架，需要建立更精确的集中性理论。

Method: 采用可交换权重的一般自助法框架，使用Chatterjee的可交换对方法研究条件于数据的集中性。通过对称群上置换游走的收敛性分析获得最优集中率。证明自助经验过程的条件期望具有自界性质，扩展了条件Rademacher平均的已知性质。

Result: 建立了自助经验过程上确界的集中不等式：一方面证明了条件于数据的集中性，另一方面证明了条件期望的集中性。这些不等式具有最优的集中率。作为应用，获得了均值向量估计的置信区域新结果，以及两样本置换检验的非渐近界。

Conclusion: 该研究为可交换权重自助法提供了系统的集中不等式理论框架，这些结果不仅具有理论意义，而且在实际统计推断中有重要应用价值，特别是在构建置信区域和进行假设检验时提供了非渐近的理论保证。

Abstract: Considering a general framework of bootstrap with exchangeable weights, we show some concentration inequalities for the supremum of the bootstrap empirical process. On the one hand, we discuss the concentration of the bootstrap empirical process around its conditional expectation with respect to the original data, and on the other hand, the concentration of the latter quantity around its mean. For the concentration conditional on data, we build on Chatterjee's exchangeable pairs approach to concentration. To attain optimal concentration rates, we develop some refined arguments for the convergence of transposition walks on the symmetric group. The conditional expectation of the bootstrap empirical process is proved to be self-bounding, thus extending a well-known property for conditional Rademacher averages. To illustrate the interest of these concentration inequalities, we provide some new results pertaining to confidence regions for the estimation of a mean vector, as well as non-asymptotic bounds for the two-sample permutation test.

</details>


### [142] [Robustified Gaussian quasi-BIC for volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29463)
*Shoichi Eguchi,Hiroki Masuda*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出非遍历连续波动率回归模型中稳健模型比较的理论框架，使用密度幂加权和Hölder不等式归一化的高斯拟似然函数，构建两种Schwarz型统计量并证明其模型选择一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在有限活动跳跃污染的非遍历连续波动率回归模型中，需要开发稳健的模型比较方法，以准确识别真实的参数化波动率系数。

Method: 使用密度幂加权和基于Hölder不等式的归一化技术改进传统高斯拟似然函数，提出两种Schwarz型统计量，并建立其模型选择一致性理论。

Result: 理论证明了所提统计量在识别最小真实参数化波动率系数方面的模型选择一致性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为非遍历连续波动率回归模型中的稳健模型比较提供了理论保证，能够有效处理跳跃污染问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical foundation for robust model comparison in a class of non-ergodic continuous volatility regression models contaminated by finite-activity jumps. Using the density-power weighting and the Hölder(-inequality)-based normalization of the conventional Gaussian quasi-likelihood function, we propose two Schwarz-type statistics and also establish their model selection consistency with respect to the minimal true parametric volatility coefficient. Numerical experiments are conducted to illustrate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [143] [Csiszár indices and interpolating copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29884)
*Cristina Butucea,Jean-François Delmas,Anne Dutfoy,Antoine Schoonaert*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了f-散度和Csiszár指数在各种一般设置下的性质，包括单调性、不变性等结构特性，并探讨了它们与随机向量copula表示的关系，构建了最小化Csiszár指数的插值copula族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究f-散度和Csiszár指数在一般凸函数和概率分布设置下的性质，特别是当边缘分布存在原子时，copula表示不唯一且变换后向量的Csiszár指数可能增加的问题，需要构建能最小化Csiszár指数并保持初始向量依赖结构的copula族。

Method: 建立了f-散度的一般结构性质，并展示了这些性质如何被相关的Csiszár指数继承；研究了Csiszár指数与随机向量copula表示的关系；构建了一个大的插值copula族来最小化Csiszár指数。

Result: 证明了f-散度和Csiszár指数的单调性和在适当变换下的不变性等结构性质；当边缘分布有原子时，copula表示不唯一且变换后向量的Csiszár指数可能增加；构建了能最小化Csiszár指数并保持初始向量依赖结构的插值copula族。

Conclusion: 论文系统建立了f-散度和Csiszár指数的一般理论框架，特别是在copula表示方面的深入分析，为解决边缘分布有原子时的依赖结构保持问题提供了有效的数学工具。

Abstract: We study various properties of $f$-divergences and Csiszár indices between two probability distributions in very general setups for the convex function $f$ and for the probability distributions. We establish general structural properties of $f$-divergences and show how they are inherited by the associated Csiszár indices, including monotonicity and invariance under suitable transformations.
  We also study the relationship between Csiszár indices and copula representations of random vectors. When the marginal distributions have atoms, the copula representation is not unique and the Csiszár index of the transformed vectors may increase. We build a large family of interpolating copulas which minimize the Csiszár index and thus preserve the dependence structure of the initial vector.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Nonnegative Matrix Factorization in the Component-Wise L1 Norm for Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29715)
*Giovanni Seraghiti,Kévin Dubrulle,Arnaud Vandaele,Nicolas Gillis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出加权L1范数非负矩阵分解(wL1-NMF)，通过加权L1范数误差度量处理含重尾噪声或异常值的数据，并开发了基于加权中位数算法的稀疏坐标下降(sCD)算法，其复杂度与数据非零元素数量成正比，适用于大规模稀疏数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准NMF使用最小二乘误差度量对重尾噪声（如拉普拉斯噪声、椒盐噪声）或异常值敏感。L1-NMF虽能处理此类噪声，但存在NP-hard问题（即使r=1），且对稀疏输入矩阵会过度强制稀疏性，当数据存在假零值时可能导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出加权L1-NMF模型，通过向与数据零值对应的WH条目添加惩罚参数来控制分解的稀疏性。开发稀疏坐标下降(sCD)算法，每个子问题通过加权中位数算法求解，复杂度与数据非零元素数量成正比，适合大规模稀疏数据。

Result: 证明了L1-NMF即使r=1也是NP-hard问题。加权L1-NMF能更好地控制稀疏性，避免过度稀疏化。sCD算法在合成和真实数据集上表现出有效性，是首个复杂度与数据非零元素数量成正比的L1-NMF算法。

Conclusion: 加权L1-NMF模型和sCD算法为处理含重尾噪声、异常值或假零值的大规模稀疏数据提供了有效解决方案，在保持可解释性的同时避免了过度稀疏化问题。

Abstract: Nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) approximates a nonnegative matrix, $X$, by the product of two nonnegative factors, $WH$, where $W$ has $r$ columns and $H$ has $r$ rows. In this paper, we consider NMF using the component-wise L1 norm as the error measure (L1-NMF), which is suited for data corrupted by heavy-tailed noise, such as Laplace noise or salt and pepper noise, or in the presence of outliers. Our first contribution is an NP-hardness proof for L1-NMF, even when $r=1$, in contrast to the standard NMF that uses least squares. Our second contribution is to show that L1-NMF strongly enforces sparsity in the factors for sparse input matrices, thereby favoring interpretability. However, if the data is affected by false zeros, too sparse solutions might degrade the model. Our third contribution is a new, more general, L1-NMF model for sparse data, dubbed weighted L1-NMF (wL1-NMF), where the sparsity of the factorization is controlled by adding a penalization parameter to the entries of $WH$ associated with zeros in the data. The fourth contribution is a new coordinate descent (CD) approach for wL1-NMF, denoted as sparse CD (sCD), where each subproblem is solved by a weighted median algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, sCD is the first algorithm for L1-NMF whose complexity scales with the number of nonzero entries in the data, making it efficient in handling large-scale, sparse data. We perform extensive numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world data to show the effectiveness of our new proposed model (wL1-NMF) and algorithm (sCD).

</details>


### [145] [Aligning Validation with Deployment: Target-Weighted Cross-Validation for Spatial Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29981)
*Alexander Brenning,Thomas Suesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标加权交叉验证(TWCV)方法，通过校准加权处理验证任务与部署任务分布不匹配问题，结合空间缓冲重采样来准确估计空间预测中的部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交叉验证假设验证任务与部署任务来自相同分布，但在空间预测等结构化数据场景中，这一假设常被违反，导致部署风险估计存在偏差。需要解决验证与部署任务分布不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出目标加权交叉验证(TWCV)：1) 通过任务描述符(如协变量和空间配置)表征预测任务；2) 使用校准加权为验证损失分配权重，使加权经验分布与目标域分布匹配；3) 结合空间缓冲重采样以增强部署分布支持集的覆盖度。

Result: 模拟研究表明，传统方法和空间估计器在不同采样场景下存在显著偏差，而缓冲TWCV在所有场景下保持近似无偏。环境污染映射案例进一步证实TWCV能更好地反映目标域预测任务。

Conclusion: 任务分布不匹配是空间预测中交叉验证偏差的主要来源，校准加权结合适当的验证任务生成器为数据集偏移下的预测风险估计提供了可行方法。

Abstract: Cross-validation (CV) is commonly used to estimate predictive risk when independent test data are unavailable. Its validity depends on the assumption that validation tasks are sampled from the same distribution as prediction tasks encountered during deployment. In spatial prediction and other settings with structured data, this assumption is frequently violated, leading to biased estimates of deployment risk. We propose Target-Weighted CV (TWCV), an estimator of deployment risk that accounts for discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions, thus accounting for (1) covariate shift and (2) task-difficulty shift. We characterize prediction tasks by descriptors such as covariates and spatial configuration. TWCV assigns weights to validation losses such that the weighted empirical distribution of validation tasks matches the corresponding distribution over a target domain. The weights are obtained via calibration weighting, yielding an importance-weighted estimator that targets deployment risk. Since TWCV requires adequate coverage of the deployment distribution's support, we combine it with spatially buffered resampling that diversifies the task difficulty distribution. In a simulation study, conventional as well as spatial estimators exhibit substantial bias depending on sampling, whereas buffered TWCV remains approximately unbiased across scenarios. A case study in environmental pollution mapping further confirms that discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions can affect performance assessment, and that buffered TWCV better reflects the prediction task over the target domain. These results establish task distribution mismatch as a primary source of CV bias in spatial prediction and show that calibration weighting combined with a suitable validation task generator provides a viable approach to estimating predictive risk under dataset shift.

</details>


### [146] [Refined Detection for Gumbel Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30017)
*Tor Lattimore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对Aaronson(2022)提出的Gumbel水印方案的简单检测机制，该机制在特定假设下被证明是接近最优的


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson(2022)提出的Gumbel水印方案需要有效的检测机制，现有检测方法可能不是最优的，需要开发更高效且理论保证的检测方法

Method: 提出了一种简单的检测机制，专门针对Gumbel水印方案设计。该机制在假设下一个令牌分布是独立同分布采样的情况下，被证明在所有模型无关的水印方案中是接近问题依赖最优的

Result: 新检测机制在理论分析中被证明是接近最优的，具有问题依赖的最优性保证，适用于模型无关的水印方案

Conclusion: 提出的简单检测机制为Gumbel水印方案提供了理论保证的接近最优检测性能，在特定假设下具有优越性

Abstract: We propose a simple detection mechanism for the Gumbel watermarking scheme proposed by Aaronson (2022). The new mechanism is proven to be near-optimal in a problem-dependent sense among all model-agnostic watermarking schemes under the assumption that the next-token distribution is sampled i.i.d.

</details>


### [147] [OneComp: One-Line Revolution for Generative AI Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28845)
*Yuma Ichikawa,Keiji Kimura,Akihiro Yoshida,Yudai Fujimoto,Hiroki Tokura,Yamato Arai,Yoshiyuki Ishii,Yusei Kawakami,Genki Shikada,Achille Jacquemond,Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Katsuki Fujisawa,Takumi Honda,Akira Sakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OneComp是一个开源压缩框架，可将专家级模型压缩工作流转化为可重复、资源自适应的流水线，自动执行混合精度分配和多阶段量化，解决基础模型部署中的内存、延迟和硬件成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署面临内存占用、延迟和硬件成本的约束。后训练压缩可以通过降低模型参数精度来缓解这些瓶颈，但实际实施存在挑战：量化算法碎片化、精度预算选择、数据驱动校准策略和硬件依赖执行机制等，导致专家工作流程复杂且难以标准化。

Method: OneComp框架采用资源自适应流水线设计：给定模型标识符和可用硬件，自动检查模型结构，规划混合精度分配，并执行渐进式量化阶段（从层级压缩到块级细化再到全局细化）。关键架构选择是将第一个量化检查点作为可部署的枢轴，确保每个后续阶段改进同一模型，质量随计算投入增加而提升。

Result: OneComp将最先进的压缩研究转化为可扩展、开源、硬件感知的流水线，弥合了算法创新与生产级模型部署之间的差距，为实际压缩工作流提供了系统化解决方案。

Conclusion: OneComp框架通过自动化、渐进式的压缩流程，解决了后训练压缩在实际部署中的碎片化挑战，使模型压缩从专家级工作流程转变为可重复、资源自适应的标准化过程，促进了基础模型的高效部署。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models is increasingly constrained by memory footprint, latency, and hardware costs. Post-training compression can mitigate these bottlenecks by reducing the precision of model parameters without significantly degrading performance; however, its practical implementation remains challenging as practitioners navigate a fragmented landscape of quantization algorithms, precision budgets, data-driven calibration strategies, and hardware-dependent execution regimes. We present OneComp, an open-source compression framework that transforms this expert workflow into a reproducible, resource-adaptive pipeline. Given a model identifier and available hardware, OneComp automatically inspects the model, plans mixed-precision assignments, and executes progressive quantization stages, ranging from layer-wise compression to block-wise refinement and global refinement. A key architectural choice is treating the first quantized checkpoint as a deployable pivot, ensuring that each subsequent stage improves the same model and that quality increases as more compute is invested. By converting state-of-the-art compression research into an extensible, open-source, hardware-aware pipeline, OneComp bridges the gap between algorithmic innovation and production-grade model deployment.

</details>


### [148] [Structural Pass Analysis in Football: Learning Pass Archetypes and Tactical Impact from Spatio-Temporal Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28916)
*Oktay Karakuş,Hasan Arkadaş*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于防守结构交互的足球传球分析框架，通过三个结构指标量化传球对防守空间配置的影响，并综合为战术影响力值(TIV)，用于识别传球类型和战术模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传球评估方法主要依赖结果指标（如得分概率或控球价值），缺乏对传球如何影响对手防守组织的洞察。需要基于防守结构交互的结构性分析框架来量化传球的战术影响。

Method: 使用同步跟踪/事件数据，开发三个互补的结构指标：线突破得分、空间增益指标和结构破坏指数，量化传球如何改变防守者的空间配置。将这些指标组合成战术影响力值(TIV)。基于2022年世界杯数据，通过无监督聚类识别传球原型，并进行空间、团队和球员层面的分析。

Result: 识别出四种可解释的传球原型：循环传球、破坏稳定传球、突破防线传球和扩展空间传球。TIV较高的传球显著更可能导致区域推进，特别是进入最后三分之一区域和禁区。团队层面分析揭示了不同的结构性传球风格，球员层面分析显示构建型后卫是结构推进的关键驱动者。传球手-接球手交互分析识别出能放大战术推进的结构性影响力传球组合。

Conclusion: 提出的框架展示了如何从跟踪数据中提取结构表征，以揭示足球中可解释的战术模式。基于防守结构交互的结构性传球分析为理解传球如何影响对手防守组织提供了新视角，补充了传统的结果导向评估方法。

Abstract: The increasing availability of spatio-temporal tracking data has created new opportunities for analysing tactical behaviour in football. However, many existing approaches evaluate passes primarily through outcome-based metrics such as scoring probability or possession value, providing limited insight into how passes influence the defensive organisation of the opponent. This paper introduces a structural framework for analysing football passes based on their interaction with defensive structure. Using synchronised tracking/event data, we derive three complementary structural metrics, Line Bypass Score, Space Gain Metric, and Structural Disruption Index, that quantify how passes alter the spatial configuration of defenders. These metrics are combined into a composite measure termed Tactical Impact Value (TIV), which captures the structural influence of individual passes. Using tracking and event data from the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we analyse structural passing behaviour across multiple tactical levels. Unsupervised clustering of structural features reveals four interpretable pass archetypes: circulatory, destabilising, line-breaking, and space-expanding passes. Empirical results show that passes with higher TIV are significantly more likely to lead to territorial progression, particularly entries into the final third and penalty box. Spatial, team-level analyses further reveal distinctive structural passing styles across teams, while player-level analysis highlights the role of build-up defenders as key drivers of structural progression. In addition, analysing passer-receiver interactions identifies structurally impactful passing partnerships that amplify tactical progression within teams. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates how structural representations derived from tracking data can reveal interpretable tactical patterns in football.

</details>


### [149] [Foundations of Polar Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28939)
*Giovanni Guasti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于极坐标几何的Polar Linear Algebra框架，将算子学习从空间域转向谱域，通过解耦谱模态实现参数减少、计算复杂度降低和模型并行化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从谱角度重新审视算子学习，旨在建立一种结合线性径向分量和周期性角度分量的结构化框架，以改善算子学习的稳定性、收敛性和可解释性

Method: 引入Polar Linear Algebra框架，基于极坐标几何定义相关算子并分析其谱特性；在MNIST基准上评估，通过施加自伴算子启发的谱约束来提升稳定性

Result: 极坐标和全谱算子能够可靠训练，谱约束改善了稳定性和收敛性；参数数量和计算复杂度降低，获得解耦谱模态的可解释表示；自然暴露了额外的模型并行化维度

Conclusion: 该工作为算子学习提供了不同的概念视角，特别适用于谱结构和并行执行至关重要的问题，通过从空间域到谱域的转换实现正交特征模态的独立计算管道

Abstract: This work revisits operator learning from a spectral perspective by introducing Polar Linear Algebra, a structured framework based on polar geometry that combines a linear radial component with a periodic angular component. Starting from this formulation, we define the associated operators and analyze their spectral properties. As a proof of feasibility, the framework is evaluated on a canonical benchmark (MNIST). Despite the simplicity of the task, the results demonstrate that polar and fully spectral operators can be trained reliably, and that imposing self-adjoint-inspired spectral constraints improves stability and convergence. Beyond accuracy, the proposed formulation leads to a reduction in parameter count and computational complexity, while providing a more interpretable representation in terms of decoupled spectral modes. By moving from a spatial to a spectral domain, the problem decomposes into orthogonal eigenmodes that can be treated as independent computational pipelines. This structure naturally exposes an additional dimension of model parallelization, complementing existing parallel strategies without relying on ad-hoc partitioning. Overall, the work offers a different conceptual lens for operator learning, particularly suited to problems where spectral structure and parallel execution are central.

</details>


### [150] [\texttt{ReproMIA}: A Comprehensive Analysis of Model Reprogramming for Proactive Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28942)
*Chihan Huang,Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReproMIA：基于模型重编程的主动式成员推断攻击框架，通过放大隐私泄露信号显著提升低误报率下的检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在关键领域广泛部署引发隐私担忧，传统成员推断攻击面临计算成本高和低误报率下性能急剧下降的限制，需要更高效的隐私审计方法

Method: 提出ReproMIA框架，利用模型重编程原理作为隐私泄露的主动信号放大器，通过主动诱导和放大模型表示中潜在的隐私足迹来增强成员推断能力

Result: 在超过10个基准测试和多种模型架构上，ReproMIA显著优于现有方法，在LLMs上平均提升5.25% AUC和10.68% TPR@1%FPR，在扩散模型上提升3.70%和12.40%

Conclusion: 模型重编程为隐私泄露检测提供了有效的主动信号放大机制，ReproMIA框架在低误报率下实现了性能突破，为深度学习模型的隐私审计提供了统一高效的新范式

Abstract: The pervasive deployment of deep learning models across critical domains has concurrently intensified privacy concerns due to their inherent propensity for data memorization. While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) serve as the gold standard for auditing these privacy vulnerabilities, conventional MIA paradigms are increasingly constrained by the prohibitive computational costs of shadow model training and a precipitous performance degradation under low False Positive Rate constraints. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel perspective by leveraging the principles of model reprogramming as an active signal amplifier for privacy leakage. Building upon this insight, we present \texttt{ReproMIA}, a unified and efficient proactive framework for membership inference. We rigorously substantiate, both theoretically and empirically, how our methodology proactively induces and magnifies latent privacy footprints embedded within the model's representations. We provide specialized instantiations of \texttt{ReproMIA} across diverse architectural paradigms, including LLMs, Diffusion Models, and Classification Models. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across more than ten benchmarks and a variety of model architectures demonstrate that \texttt{ReproMIA} consistently and substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a transformative leap in performance specifically within low-FPR regimes, such as an average of 5.25\% AUC and 10.68\% TPR@1\%FPR increase over the runner-up for LLMs, as well as 3.70\% and 12.40\% respectively for Diffusion Models.

</details>


### [151] [Differentiable Initialization-Accelerated CPU-GPU Hybrid Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28943)
*Mingju Liu,Jiaqi Yin,Alvaro Velasquez,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合CPU-GPU框架，通过可微分预求解为ILP求解器提供高质量初始解，在组合调度问题上实现10倍性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合调度问题通常表述为整数线性规划（ILP），但由于NP难特性，在大规模场景下最优求解仍然面临挑战。现有ILP求解器在处理大规模调度问题时效率有限，需要新的方法来加速求解过程。

Method: 提出混合CPU-GPU框架，结合可微分优化与经典ILP求解。使用可微分预求解快速生成高质量部分解，作为商业ILP求解器（CPLEX、Gurobi）和开源求解器HiGHS的热启动。该方法通过GPU加速的预求解显著改善早期剪枝效果。

Result: 在工业级基准测试中，相比最先进的独立求解器，实现了高达10倍的性能提升，最优性差距缩小到<0.1%。这是首次展示利用可微分优化初始化精确ILP求解器用于组合调度问题。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将机器学习基础设施与经典精确优化方法集成的潜力，为更广泛领域的优化问题提供了新的解决思路，开辟了可微分优化与精确求解器结合的新方向。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid CPU-GPU framework for solving combinatorial scheduling problems formulated as Integer Linear Programming (ILP). While scheduling underpins many optimization tasks in computing systems, solving these problems optimally at scale remains a long-standing challenge due to their NP-hard nature. We introduce a novel approach that combines differentiable optimization with classical ILP solving. Specifically, we utilize differentiable presolving to rapidly generate high-quality partial solutions, which serve as warm-starts for commercial ILP solvers (CPLEX, Gurobi) and rising open-source solver HiGHS. This method enables significantly improved early pruning compared to state-of-the-art standalone solvers. Empirical results across industry-scale benchmarks demonstrate up to a $10\times$ performance gain over baselines, narrowing the optimality gap to $<0.1\%$. This work represents the first demonstration of utilizing differentiable optimization to initialize exact ILP solvers for combinatorial scheduling, opening new opportunities to integrate machine learning infrastructure with classical exact optimization methods across broader domains.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-Agent LLMs for Adaptive Acquisition in Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28959)
*Andrea Carbonati,Mohammadsina Almasi,Hadis Anahideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体框架解决LLM在优化任务中的探索-利用权衡问题，通过将策略控制与候选生成分离，显著提升LLM搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在序列决策和黑盒优化中的探索-利用权衡机制尚不明确，不同于贝叶斯优化有明确的采集函数，LLM基于提示的隐式推理使得搜索行为难以分析或控制。

Method: 提出多智能体框架，将探索-利用控制分解为策略调解和候选生成：策略智能体为多个搜索标准分配可解释权重，生成智能体基于权重定义的搜索策略生成候选解。

Result: 实验结果表明，将策略控制与候选生成分离显著提高了LLM介导搜索的有效性，多智能体框架相比单智能体方法表现出更稳定的搜索动态和更好的优化性能。

Conclusion: 通过将探索-利用决策显式化、可观察和可调整，多智能体分解方法有效解决了LLM在优化任务中的认知过载问题，为LLM介导搜索提供了更可控的框架。

Abstract: The exploration-exploitation trade-off is central to sequential decision-making and black-box optimization, yet how Large Language Models (LLMs) reason about and manage this trade-off remains poorly understood. Unlike Bayesian Optimization, where exploration and exploitation are explicitly encoded through acquisition functions, LLM-based optimization relies on implicit, prompt-based reasoning over historical evaluations, making search behavior difficult to analyze or control.
